HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000225/B001","root_000225/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2446cdfb518f05c83cf2","text":"2. **The terrifying counter-image of “more.”** Fixed `ج ح م B001` explicitly includes an abundance of embers, while contextual `ك ث ر B001` in 102:1 names numerical or material increase and `B002` names rivalry through having more. The sequence carries the audience from competitive abundance to the sight of a massed blaze. Piled embers are assigned the role of a material revaluation of accumulation. I supply the transformation arrow: the packet does not say that amassed goods become embers, only that one abundance is answered by another. Under this activation, what they see is not just fire but an inverted surplus in which “more” has become the horror rather than the advantage.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000225/B003","root_000531/B001","root_001069/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_276e0860e136cb3f0d52","text":"2. **The fire as an eye that stares back.** Branch evidence: ج ح م B003, glowing or staring eye; ر ء ي B001, seeing; ع ي ن B001/B002, eye and face-to-face seeing. Structural cue: the next ayah explicitly adds \"عين اليقين\" to the seeing of this object. Assigned role: جحيم is not only seen; its own branch inventory contains a staring-eye image. Abductive move: I supply the reversal that the object of sight becomes a gaze directed back at the viewers. Reading change: the ayah reads as exposure under a fiery eye, where the seers are also seen.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000225/B001","root_000531/B001","root_001040/B001","root_001696/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3e7b6ff04762d110b5ac","text":"1. **Compulsory sensory disclosure.** Branch evidence: ر ء ي B001, seeing by eye or insight; ج ح م B001, blazing fire and intense heat; ع ل م B001, knowing; ي ق ن B001, settled certainty. Structural cue: the sequence moves from \"you will know\" to \"you will surely see.\" Assigned role: رأي converts knowledge into perception; جحيم supplies the object whose heat cannot be kept conceptual. Abductive move: I infer that later seeing verifies the prior warning by forcing contact with its object. Reading change: the ayah reads as compulsory presentation of evidence, not just a threat-image.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000225/B003","root_000531/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_407dc6e6b6d1f11fdf48","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:6:1\",\"root_000531\",[\"B004\"]],[\"102:6:2\",\"root_000225\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ateş yalnız görülen bir nesne değildir; bakanlara geri bakan bir göz gibi onları da görünür ve gözetlenen hale getirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000225/B004","root_000225/B005","root_000531/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_437bfb92edc1a19321f8","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:6:1\",\"root_000531\",[\"B005\"]],[\"102:6:2\",\"root_000225\",[\"B004\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"İnsanlara gösterilmek üzere yönetilen rekabetçi görünüş, artık yönetilemeyen bir maruziyete ve arkasındaki öfke ile utanmazlığın açığa çıkmasına dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000225/B001","root_000531/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4dadf87ad7c3ebcec876","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:6:1\",\"root_000531\",[\"B001\"]],[\"102:6:2\",\"root_000225\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Görme, önceki uyarıları uzaktan bilinen düşünceler olmaktan çıkarıp kaçınılamayan duyusal delile dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000225/B004","root_000225/B005","root_000531/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6939f1621603a1af22ff","text":"4. **Shameless display becomes exposed heat.** Branch evidence: ج ح م B004, face flaring with anger; ج ح م B005, little shame/modesty; ر ء ي B005, showing off before people; ك ث ر B003, claims/status. Structural cue: earlier abundance can be socially displayed; now the object of seeing is a blaze connected to face and shame branches. Assigned role: جحم B004/B005 turns display into exposed emotional/moral heat. Abductive move: I infer that what was performed for others returns as a state that cannot be modestly hidden. Reading change: \"you will see the blazing fire\" also reads as the exposure of the affective heat behind competitive display.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000225/B002","root_001286/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_831427aa149c12f69bef","text":"3. **Rivalry becomes a battlefield blaze.** Branch evidence: ج ح م B002, battle or death at a blaze; ك ث ر B002, rivalry in abundance; ق ب ر B001, graves. Structural cue: competitive accumulation leads through graves to الجحيم. Assigned role: تكاثر supplies social contest; جحم B002 supplies the violent heat of contest when unveiled. Abductive move: I infer that rivalry's underlying warlike structure is made manifest as blazing conflict/death. Reading change: the fire is not only punishment after rivalry; it is rivalry's inner violence made visible.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B001","root_001040/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_984621cf04767015de0c","text":"1. **Knowledge converted into unavoidable sensory exposure.** Fixed `لَتَرَوُنَّ` anchors `ر ء ي B001` (seeing by eye or insight), and fixed `ٱلْجَحِيمَ` anchors `ج ح م B001` (blazing fire, piled embers, and severe heat). Repeated `ع ل م B001` and `ي ق ن B001` immediately precede the emphatic visual verb; 102:7 then repeats it with `عَيْنَ ٱلْيَقِينِ`. Sight is assigned the role of verification, while the blaze supplies an object forceful enough to defeat prior diversion. I infer that sensory exposure closes the gap left by nonoperative knowledge. The ayah therefore reads as compelled contact with what warning had made cognitively available, not as detached observation from a safe distance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000225/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a433698195646d4518b6","text":"102:7 strongly changed this ayah by attaching \"عين اليقين\" to the same object, making the ج ح م B003 \"staring eye\" branch unexpectedly central rather than ornamental. The fire is no longer just something seen; it participates in a field of seeing. 102:8 then shifted the fire from spectacle to evidence: after seeing comes questioning, so the blaze functions as disclosed proof in an accountability sequence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000225/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a55f5d20d7d3431789c9","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:6:2\",\"root_000225\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Rekabetin örtülü savaşçılığı, ateşin çatışma ve ölüm taşıyan hararetinde görünür hale gelir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000225/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b222edc5eb6813051c8e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:6:2\",\"root_000225\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Çoklukta aranan yığılma, korların yığılmış bolluğunda korkutucu bir karşı biçim kazanır; daha fazla artık üstünlük değil dehşettir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c528663c8a3ff97a916d","text":"4. **The reversal of managed public display.** Fixed `ر ء ي B005` is the dormant branch of acting so that people will see, activated by contextual `ك ث ر B002` with its boasting and status-rivalry. Competitive abundance is assigned the role of an earlier spectacle managed for human observers; the emphatic fixed verb assigns compulsory vision to those performers. I infer that rivalry operates through public display, a social mechanism supported but not explicitly asserted by the packet. `لَتَرَوُنَّ` consequently reverses the visual economy: those who sought advantage by controlling what others saw are made into viewers of an object they cannot stage or edit.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000225/B003","root_000531/B004","root_001069/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c6bf41eb4bb752394f53","text":"3. **A gaze that confronts the seer in return.** Fixed `ج ح م B003` preserves the image of a glowing, wide, sharply staring eye; fixed `ر ء ي B004` supplies mutual visibility and facing. The next ayah's explicit `عَيْنَ` (`ع ي ن B001/B002`) activates both dormant branches, while the immediate repetition of `لَتَرَوُنَّ` holds the parties face to face. The staring-eye image is assigned to the blaze and mutual visibility to the encounter. I infer the personifying arrow by which the seen fire effectively “looks back”; no grammatical agent states this. The ayah then reads as confrontation and exposure: the viewers do not retain the one-way control normally implied by looking at an object.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000225/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d610bfc8f2c4dee417ca","text":"102:7's explicit `عَيْنَ` produced the clearest surprise, activating fixed `ج ح م B003` as a staring eye and fixed `ر ء ي B004` as mutual visibility; the blaze became a confronting presence, not a passive spectacle. 102:8 then corrected any reading of sight as the endpoint: the visual exposure serves as an evidentiary prelude to questioning.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
